**步骤5: write_docx**
- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\format_analysis.py", "content": "from docx import Document\nfrom collections import Counter\nimport re\nimport string\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n# ==================== 第一步：读取文档并分析格式 ====================\ndoc_path = r\"E:\\test_dir\\test.docx\"\ndoc = Document(doc_path)\n\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"【阶段一：文档格式分析】\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# 统计基本信息\nparagraphs = doc.paragraphs\ntables = doc.tables\ntotal_chars = sum(len(p.text) for p in paragraphs)\n\nprint(f\"\\n📄 文档基本信息：\")\nprint(f\"   - 段落总数：{len(paragraphs)}\")\nprint(f\"   - 表格总数：{len(tables)}\")\nprint(f\"   - 总字符数：{total_chars}\")\nprint(f\"   - 标题列表：[] (无标题样式)\")\n\n# 分析格式信息\nformat_info = []\nfor i, para in enumerate(paragraphs):\n    print(f\"\\n📝 段落 {i+1}: \\\"{para.text}\\\"\")\n    print(f\"   - 样式: {para.style.name}\")\n    print(f\"   - 对齐方式: {para.alignment}\")\n    print(f\"   - 缩进: left={para.paragraph_format.left_indent}, right={para.paragraph_format.right_indent}\")\n    \n    if para.runs:\n        # 收集所有字体信息\n        fonts = set()\n        sizes = set()\n        bolds = set()\n        itals = set()\n        for run in para.runs:\n            font = run.font\n            fonts.add(font.name)\n            sizes.add(font.size)\n            bolds.add(font.bold)\n            itals.add(font.italic)\n        \n        print(f\"   - 字体: {fonts}\")\n        print(f\"   - 字号: {sizes}\")\n        print(f\"   - 加粗: {bolds}\")\n        print(f\"   - 斜体: {itals}\")\n    \n    format_info.append({\n        \"index\": i + 1,\n        \"text\": para.text,\n        \"style\": para.style.name,\n        \"alignment\": str(para.alignment),\n        \"left_indent\": str(para.paragraph_format.left_indent),\n        \"right_indent\": str(para.paragraph_format.right_indent),\n        \"top_spacing\": str(para.paragraph_format.space_before),\n        \"bottom_spacing\": str(para.paragraph_format.space_after),\n        \"line_spacing\": str(para.paragraph_format.line_spacing),\n        \"fonts\": list(fonts) if para.runs else [],\n        \"sizes\": list(sizes) if para.runs else [],\n    })\n\n# 输出格式特征总结\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"【格式特征汇总】\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nall_fonts = set()\nall_sizes = set()\nfor info in format_info:\n    all_fonts.update(info[\"fonts\"])\n    all_sizes.update(info[\"sizes\"])\n\nprint(f\"使用的字体: {all_fonts if all_fonts else '未检测到特殊字体'}\")\nprint(f\"使用的字号: {all_sizes if all_sizes else '未检测到特殊字号'}\")\nprint(f\"对齐方式: {[info['alignment'] for info in format_info]}\")\nprint(f\"行间距: {[info['line_spacing'] for info in format_info]}\")\n\n# ==================== 第二步：词频统计 ====================\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"【词频统计分析】\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\n# 提取所有文本\nfull_text = \"\\n\".join([p.text for p in paragraphs])\nprint(f\"\\n原始全文: \\\"{full_text}\\\"\")\n\n# 分词处理（英文按空格分，中文需要jieba但这里主要是英文）\nwords = full_text.lower().split()\n# 去除标点符号\ncleaned_words = [re.sub(r'[^\\w]', '', w) for w in words]\ncleaned_words = [w for w in cleaned_words if w]\n\nprint(f\"\\n分词结果: {cleaned_words}\")\n\n# 统计词频\nword_counter = Counter(cleaned_words)\nprint(f\"\\n完整词频统计:\")\nfor word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n    print(f\"   '{word}': {count}次\")\n\n# 取前10\ntop10 = word_counter.most_common(10)\nprint(f\"\\n词频TOP10:\")\nfor rank, (word, count) in enumerate(top10, 1):\n    print(f\"   {rank}. '{word}': {count}次\")\n\n# 保存词频到文件\noutput_path = r\"E:\\test_dir\\word_freq.txt\"\nwith open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    f.write(\"=\" * 60 + \"\\n\")\n    f.write(\"词频统计报告\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"生成时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"源文件: test.docx\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"总词数: {len(cleaned_words)}\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"去重词数: {len(word_counter)}\\n\")\n    f.write(\"=\" * 60 + \"\\n\\n\")\n    f.write(\"词频TOP10:\\n\")\n    f.write(\"-\" * 40 + \"\\n\")\n    for rank, (word, count) in enumerate(top10, 1):\n        f.write(f\"{rank:>3}. {word:<15} {count}次\\n\")\n    f.write(\"\\n全部词频:\\n\")\n    f.write(\"-\" * 40 + \"\\n\")\n    for word, count in word_counter.most_common():\n        f.write(f\"{word:<15} {count}次\\n\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n✅ 词频统计已保存到: {output_path}\")\n\n# 同时保存格式分析结果\nformat_path = r\"E:\\test_dir\\format_analysis_result.txt\"\nwith open(format_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    f.write(\"=\" * 60 + \"\\n\")\n    f.write(\"文档格式分析报告\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"生成时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"源文件: test.docx\\n\")\n    f.write(\"=\" * 60 + \"\\n\\n\")\n    f.write(\"基本信息:\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"  段落数: {len(paragraphs)}\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"  表格数: {len(tables)}\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"  总字符数: {total_chars}\\n\\n\")\n    f.write(\"格式详情:\\n\")\n    for info in format_info:\n        f.write(f\"  段落{info['index']}: \\\"{info['text']}\\\"\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"    样式: {info['style']}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"    对齐: {info['alignment']}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"    字体: {info['fonts']}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"    字号: {info['sizes']}\\n\")\n        f.write(f\"    行间距: {info['line_spacing']}\\n\\n\")\n\nprint(f\"✅ 格式分析结果已保存到: {format_path}\")"}`

- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\create_pdf.py", "content": "from fpdf import FPDF\nimport os\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nclass PDFReport(FPDF):\n    def header(self):\n        self.set_font('Helvetica', 'B', 12)\n        self.cell(0, 10, '文档分析与增强报告', 0, 1, 'C')\n        self.line(10, self.get_y(), 200, self.get_y())\n        self.ln(5)\n\n    def footer(self):\n        self.set_y(-15)\n        self.set_font('Helvetica', 'I', 8)\n        self.cell(0, 10, f'页码 {self.page_no()}/{{nb}}', 0, 0, 'C')\n\n# 创建PDF\npdf = PDFReport()\npdf.alias_nb_pages()\npdf.set_auto_page_break(auto=True, margin=20)\npdf.add_page()\n\n# 设置字体\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', '', 11)\n\n# 1. 原始内容\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', 'B', 14)\npdf.cell(0, 10, '1. 原始内容', ln=True)\npdf.ln(3)\n\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', 'B', 12)\npdf.cell(0, 8, '1.1 主题分类', ln=True)\npdf.ln(2)\n\n# 主题分类表格\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', '', 10)\npdf.set_fill_color(200, 220, 255)\npdf.cell(40, 8, '类别', border=1, fill=True)\npdf.cell(40, 8, '原文', border=1, fill=True)\npdf.cell(80, 8, '主题', border=1, fill=True, ln=True)\n\nrows = [\n    ('测试相关', 'E2E test', '测试/验收'),\n    ('文档相关', 'document', '文档管理'),\n    ('内容相关', 'content', '内容描述'),\n]\nfor cat, orig, theme in rows:\n    pdf.set_fill_color(245, 245, 245)\n    pdf.cell(40, 8, cat, border=1, fill=True)\n    pdf.cell(40, 8, orig, border=1, fill=True)\n    pdf.cell(80, 8, theme, border=1, fill=True, ln=True)\n\npdf.ln(5)\n\n# 重组段落\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', 'B', 12)\npdf.cell(0, 8, '1.2 重组后的段落', ln=True)\npdf.ln(2)\n\nparagraphs = [\n    ('段落组A - 测试与验收', 'E2E（端到端）测试是软件测试的重要组成部分'),\n    ('段落组B - 文档管理', 'document 代表文档的核心概念'),\n    ('段落组C - 内容描述', 'content 表示文档承载的具体信息'),\n]\nfor title, text in paragraphs:\n    pdf.set_font('Helvetica', 'B', 10)\n    pdf.cell(0, 7, f'  {title}', ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font('Helvetica', '', 10)\n    pdf.cell(0, 7, f'    {text}', ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(2)\n\n# 2. 词频统计\npdf.ln(5)\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', 'B', 14)\npdf.cell(0, 10, '2. 词频统计', ln=True)\npdf.ln(3)\n\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', 'B', 12)\npdf.cell(0, 8, '2.1 词频TOP10', ln=True)\npdf.ln(2)\n\npdf.set_font('Helvetica', '', 10)\npdf.set_fill_color(200, 220, 255)\npdf.cell(20, 8, '排名', border=1, fill=True)\npdf.cell(60, 8, '单词', border=1, fill=True)\npdf.cell(90, 8, '出现次数', border=1, fill=True, ln=True)\n\nwords = [('1', 'e2e', '1次'), ('2', 'test', '1次'), \n         ('3', 'document', '1次'), ('4', 'content', '1次')]\nfor rank, word, count in words:\n    pdf.set_fill_color(245, 245, 245)\n    pdf.cell(20, 8, rank, border=1, fill=True)\n    pdf.cell(60, 8, word, border=1, fill=True)\n    pdf.cell(90, 8, count, border=1, fill=True, - 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\verify_pdf.py", "content": "import os\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\npdf_path = r\"E:\\test_dir\\output.pdf\"\n\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\nprint(\"【PDF验证报告】\")\nprint(\"=\" * 60)\n\nresults = []\n\n# 检查1：文件是否存在\nif os.path.exists(pdf_path):\n    results.append((\"文件存在\", \"PASS\"))\n    print(f\"\\n✅ 文件存在: {pdf_path}\")\nelse:\n    results.append((\"文件存在\", \"FAIL\"))\n    print(f\"\\n❌ 文件不存在: {pdf_path}\")\n    with open(r\"E:\\test_dir\\pdf_verify.txt\", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(\"PDF验证失败：文件不存在\")\n    exit(1)\n\n# 检查2：文件大小\nsize = os.path.getsize(pdf_path)\nif size > 1024:\n    results.append((\"文件大小\", f\"PASS ({size} bytes > 1KB)\"))\n    print(f\"✅ 文件大小: {size} bytes ({size/1024:.2f} KB) > 1KB\")\nelse:\n    results.append((\"文件大小\", f\"FAIL ({size} bytes <= 1KB)\"))\n    print(f\"❌ 文件大小: {size} bytes (应大于1KB)\")\n\n# 检查3：文件头签名\nwith open(pdf_path, 'rb') as f:\n    header = f.read(5)\n    expected = b'%PDF-'\n    if header == expected:\n        results.append((\"PDF签名\", \"PASS\"))\n        print(f\"✅ PDF文件头正确: %PDF-\")\n    else:\n        results.append((\"PDF签名\", f\"FAIL (实际: {header})\"))\n        print(f\"❌ PDF文件头不正确: {header}\")\n\n# 写入验证结果\nverify_path = r\"E:\\test_dir\\pdf_verify.txt\"\nwith open(verify_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    f.write(\"=\" * 60 + \"\\n\")\n    f.write(\"PDF文件验证报告\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"生成时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\\n\")\n    f.write(f\"验证文件: output.pdf\\n\")\n    f.write(\"=\" * 60 + \"\\n\\n\")\n    \n    for check, result in results:\n        status = \"✅ PASS\" if \"PASS\" in result else \"❌ FAIL\"\n        f.write(f\"{check}: {status} - {result}\\n\")\n    \n    f.write(f\"\\n总结: {sum(1 for _, r in results if 'PASS' in r)}/{len(results)} 项通过\\n\")\n\nprint(f\"\\n✅ 验证结果已保存到: {verify_path}\")"}`

六、结论
Notepad是一个轻量级文本编辑器，对系统资源影响极小。
从数据来看，启动Notepad后系统资源使用率反而略有下降，这可能是因为系统自动回收了闲置内存
，或其他后台进程退出。
建议：Notepad对系统性能几乎无影响，可以放心使用。

RGBColor\n\ndoc = Document()\n\n# 标题\nstyle = doc.styles['Title']\nstyle.font.size = Pt(20)\nstyle.font.bold = True\nstyle.font.color.rgb = RGBColor(0, 51, 102)\ndoc.add_heading('多阶段文档处理任务执行报告（完整版）', level=0)\n\nnow = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\ndoc.add_paragraph(f'执行人: 欧阳  |  时间: {now}  |  版本: v1.0 完整版')\n\n# 一、任务执行概览\ndoc.add_heading('一、任务执行概览', level=1)\n\nt1 = doc.add_table(rows=9, cols=4)\nt1.style = 'Medium Shading 1 Accent 1'\nfor i, h in enumerate(['阶段', '步骤', '状态', '详情']):\n    t1.rows[0].cells[i].text = h\n\nsteps = [\n    ('阶段一', '①读取文档', '✅', '1段,
